# Signal Strive — Deep Analysis v3

**Generated:** 2026-07-08 18:19  
**Method:** `side`+position-state fill classification (exit_reason is always `''` — not usable)  
**Strats:** 33  (excluded #35 BNB + #36 SOL — 2020-2021 only data)  

---

## Overall Fill Statistics

| Metric | Value |
|--------|-------|
| Total Local Entries (capped ≤2026-05-31) | 7,361 |
| Total Local Exits  (capped ≤2026-05-31) | 7,354 |
| **Total SS Fills (raw)** | **13,103** |
| **Total SS Entry Fills** | **6,657**  (90.4% of local entries) |
| **Total SS Exit Fills**  | **6,446**  (87.7% of local exits) |
| Avg Entry Diff — NEW side-based  | **3.422%** |
| Avg Entry Diff — OLD sequential  | 8.552% (inflated by pairing shift) |
| Avg Exit Diff  — side-based      | 11.705% |

> **Why old sequential was wrong:** When SS skips a fill, the alternating (entry,exit,entry,exit...)
> index assumptions shift — local entry N gets compared to exit fill N or entry fill N+1,
> causing artificially large diffs. Side-based classification uses SS's own `side` field
> (`buy`/`sell`) + position-state tracking to correctly label each fill as entry or exit.
